1 |

7 [ 1 ] praipādhyāyaḥ
(a) hotā yakṣadagniṃ samidhā supamidhā samiddhaṃ nābhā pṛthivyāḥ saṃgathe vārmasya |
varṣman divaṃ īḻapade vaiśvājyasya hotaryaṃja ||
(ā) hotā yakṣattanūnapātarmaditergarbhaṃ bhuvanasya gopām |
medhyārthaṃ devo devebhyo devyānān pathau anaktu vaiśvājyasya hotaryaṃja ||
(i) hotā yakṣannarāśaṃsaṃ nṛśaṃsaṃ naiḥ praṇetram |
gobhirpuṃvāān syādiraiḥ śakttīvān rathaiḥ prathamayāṃvā hiraṇyaiścandrī vaiśvājyasya hotaryaṃja ||
(u) hotā yakṣadagnimīḻa īḻito devo devā ā vakṣadūtau havyavāḻmūraḥ |
upemaṃ yajñamupemāṃ devo devahūtimavatu vaiśvājyasya hotaryaṃja ||
(e) hotā yakṣadbarhiḥ suṣṭarīmorṇamadā asmin yajñe tri ca pra ca prathatāṃ svāsthaṃ devebhyaḥ |
aimenadarva vasavo rudrā ādityāḥ sadanu priyaṃmindrasyāstu vaiśvājyasya hotaryaṃja ||
(ai) hotā yakṣadura ṛṣvaḥ kavaṣyau kaupadhāvanīrudātābhirjihatāṃ tri pakṣobhiḥ śrayantām |
suprāyaṇā asmin yajñe tri śrayantāmṛtāvṛdho vyantvājyasya hotaryaṃja ||
(o) hotā yakṣaduṣasānktā bṛhatī supeśasā naiḥ paṃktibhyo yoniṃ kṛṇvānai |
saṃsmyamāne indreṇa devaireṃdaṃ barhiḥ sīdatāṃ vītāṃājyasya hotaryaṃja ||

(a) devaṃ barhi: sudevaṃ devai: syātsuvīraṃ vīrairvastorṭujyetākto: pradhriyetālyān rāyā barhiṣmato madema vasuvane vasudheyasya vetu yaja |

(i) devī upāsānaktā vyasmin yajñe pratyaddhetāmapi nūnaṃ daivīrviśa: prāyāsiṣṭāṃ suprīte sudhite vasuvane vasudheyasya vītāṃ yaja |
(u) devī joṣṭrī vasudhitī yayoranyāghā dveṣāṃsi yūyavadānyāvakṣadasu vāryāṇi yajamānāya vasuvane vasudheyasya vītāṃ yaja |
